- **goalscorer-prop-modeling**: Models anytime goalscorer probabilities for soccer players and compares them with sportsbook prices: expected minutes and start probability, non-penalty xG per 90 with shrinkage, penalty duty, team goal expectation from the match market, one-sided price handling, voids, and backtests with get_prop_history.
